> DEVELOPER

Lane B Single-thread Disciplined。
  ▎
  ▎ 背景
  ▎
  ▎ 东方红重写项目。六个 feature 的 core 层已通过交叉审查（frame/connection/receive/send/task/settings 全部 A/A-）。现在推进 runtime 编排层，把六个 feature 的公开 API 串成可运行的数据通路。
  ▎
  ▎ runtime 已有骨架（rewrite/src/runtime/index.ts），但当前只聚合 frame/settings/storage-local-baseline 给首页 overview，核心事件通路未接。
  ▎
  ▎ 直接合同
  ▎
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-thin-ui-runtime-wiring.md（runtime 职责定义、层级消费规则）
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md（4.1-4.6 全部交互对）
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-boundaries.md（feature owner 和 public API）
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md（分层架构）
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-connection-platform-bridge.md（connection 与 platform 的桥接）
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md（目录结构 canonical）
  ▎ - 六个 feature 的 index.ts（public API surface）
  ▎
  ▎ 边界护栏
  ▎
  ▎ - runtime 不拥有任何 feature 内部业务 state、领域算法或协议语义
  ▎ - runtime 只做装配、生命周期、事件路由、边界例外登记
  ▎ - runtime 不引入 Pinia 或全局 store 作为业务中心
  ▎ - pages/widgets/runtime 只从 feature root import，禁止 feature 子目录穿透
  ▎ - 不接 platform/preload/main 的真实能力，只用 adapter interface 的 fake/mock
  ▎ - 不做 UI
  ▎
  ▎ 第一步：全面摸底（开 3 个 explore 子 agent 并行）
  ▎
  ▎ Agent A：现有 runtime 代码 + 相关测试
  ▎
  ▎ 通读以下文件，理解当前骨架做了什么、缺什么：
  ▎ - rewrite/src/runtime/index.ts
  ▎ - rewrite/src/runtime/__tests__/rewrite-runtime.spec.ts
  ▎ - rewrite/src/app/ 全部文件
  ▎ - rewrite/src/pages/ 全部文件
  ▎ 产出：当前 runtime 覆盖了哪些能力，哪些数据通路完全缺失。
  ▎
  ▎ Agent B：六个 feature 的 public API surface
  ▎
  ▎ 通读六个 feature 的 index.ts，列出每个 feature 暴露了哪些可被 runtime 消费的公开入口：
  ▎ - Service 工厂和 command/query 方法
  ▎ - Selector 函数
  ▎ - Adapter port 接口（用于 runtime 注入 fake/real 实现）
  ▎ - Reader 接口（只读查询）
  ▎ 产出：一份完整的 public API 清单，按 feature 分组。
  ▎
  ▎ Agent C：架构文档中的 runtime 职责和交互要求
  ▎
  ▎ 通读以下文档，提取 runtime 必须履行的职责清单：
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-thin-ui-runtime-wiring.md 全文
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md Section 4 全部交互对，标注哪些需要 runtime orchestration
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md 中 runtime 相关段落
  ▎ - codestable/architecture/rewrite-connection-platform-bridge.md
  ▎ 产出：runtime 必须实现的编排点清单（启动顺序、事件路由、生命周期管理、边界例外）。
  ▎
  ▎ 第二步：主线程汇总与规划
  ▎
  ▎ 汇总三个 agent 的结果，产出 runtime 扩展方案：
  ▎
  ▎ 1. 数据通路定义：明确 runtime 需要接通的核心链路
  ▎   - connection → receive（入站字节流注入）
  ▎   - receive → task（触发条件匹配）
  ▎   - task → send（定时/条件触发发送请求）
  ▎   - send → connection（通过 adapter 写出）
  ▎   - settings → 各 feature（配置快照分发）
  ▎ 2. 模块拆分方案：当前 runtime/index.ts 只有一个聚合函数，需要按职责拆分
  ▎   - runtime/lifecycle.ts — 启动/销毁顺序
  ▎   - runtime/event-router.ts — 跨 feature 事件路由
  ▎   - runtime/feature-wiring.ts — feature service/adapter/context 装配
  ▎   - runtime/index.ts — facade 入口
  ▎ 3. 实施批次：按依赖顺序拆 2-3 个独立批次
  ▎
  ▎ 如果需要更正式的 design 阶段，调用 /cs-feat-design 为 runtime 产出正式 design doc。如果改动范围足够清晰，直接按本提示词推进。
  ▎
  ▎ 第三步：实施（开 executor 子 agent 并行）
  ▎
  ▎ 按批次实施。每个批次完成后跑 pnpm -C rewrite build + pnpm -C rewrite lint。
  ▎
  ▎ 核心约束：
  ▎ - runtime 文件中不得 import 任何 feature 的 core/state/services 内部路径，只从 feature root index.ts import
  ▎ - runtime 不得持有可变业务 state，只持有对 feature service/reader 的引用
  ▎ - 所有跨 feature 事件流必须通过 feature 公开的 callback/event/selector 机制，不能 runtime 直接操作 feature 内部
  ▎ - 新增代码必须有对应测试（至少覆盖 happy path 和 wiring 正确性）
  ▎
  ▎ 第四步：验证
  ▎
  ▎ 开 verifier 子 agent 检查：
  ▎ - runtime 是否只从 feature root import
  ▎ - 是否有任何 feature 内部路径穿透
  ▎ - 测试是否覆盖所有新增数据通路
  ▎ - build + lint 通过
  ▎ - 与 thin-ui-runtime-wiring.md 的职责定义一致
  ▎
  ▎ 输出要求
  ▎
  ▎ 实施摘要必须包含：
  ▎ - Changed files: 改了什么文件、改了什么
  ▎ - Verify evidence: 跑了什么验证命令、结果是什么
  ▎ - Open issues: 还有什么未决问题
  ▎
  ▎ 可用 skill
  ▎
  ▎ - /cs-feat-design：如果第二步发现需要正式 design doc
  ▎ - /cs-feat-impl：如果有 checklist 要逐项推进
  ▎ - /cs-explore：代码探索（替代手动 explore agent）
  ▎
  ▎ 硬约束
  ▎
  ▎ - runtime/ 下所有 .ts 文件零 Vue/Pinia 依赖（runtime 是纯 TS 编排层）
  ▎ - 每个 runtime 模块不超过 200 行
  ▎ - 不引入新的 global store
  ▎ - 不暴露裸 IPC、Electron、Node 能力

## Findings

根据六个 feature 的 index.ts，runtime 可消费的公开入口如下：

### 1. frame（`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/index.ts`）

**1. Service 工厂和 command/query 方法**
- `createFrameAssetReader()` → 创建 Reader 实例

**2. Selector 函数**
- `findFrameAssets(query)` → 查找帧资产
- `getFrameAsset(id)` → 获取单个帧资产
- `getSelectedFrameAsset()` → 获取当前选中帧资产
- `listFrameAssetSummaries()` → 列出帧资产摘要
- `selectFieldReferenceOptions(query)` → 选择字段引用选项
- `selectFrameReferenceOptions(query)` → 选择帧引用选项

**3. Adapter port 接口**
- 无（Reader 为具体工厂返回，未暴露接口类型）

**4. Reader 接口**

**5. Context 或其他公开类型**

## Impact
- **Scope**: multi-file（跨 6 个 feature root public API）
- **Risk**: low（纯读操作，无代码修改）
- **Affected areas**: runtime 装配层需要根据这些入口进行依赖注入和编排

## Relationships
- **共同模式**：所有 feature 均采用 `create*Service(options)` 工厂 + `create*Reader()` 分离读写能力的模式
- **Selector 统一性**：所有 feature 均导出 `select*` 函数用于 Pinia store 查询
- **类型导出**：所有 feature 均导出核心类型（`*Snapshot`, `*Config`, `*Status`, `*Result` 等）供 runtime 使用
- **特殊性**：task feature 未导出 `createTaskReader()`，只导出 `TaskReader` 类型；frame feature 只导出 Reader 无 Service

## Recommendation
- runtime 装配层应优先使用 `create*Service(options)` 工厂创建 service 实例，并通过对应的 `Create*ServiceOptions` 注入依赖（如 platform facade、其他 feature 的 Reader）
- 只读查询场景可使用 `create*Reader()` 或直接调用 `select*` 函数
- task feature 如需独立 Reader 实例，需检查 services 实现是否支持

## Next Steps
- 如需深入某个 feature 的具体 command/query 方法签名，可读取对应的 `services.ts` 文件
- 如需了解 selector 的具体参数和返回类型，可读取对应的 `selectors.ts` 文件
- 如需规划 runtime 装配顺序，建议先梳理 feature 间依赖关系（如 send 依赖 connection，task 依赖 send 等）

## Findings
- **Files**: [/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/architecture/rewrite-thin-ui-runtime-wiring.md, /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md, /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md, /mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/architecture/rewrite-connection-platform-bridge.md]
- **Root cause**: Runtime 职责在四份架构文档中分散定义，需系统性提取启动顺序、事件路由、生命周期管理、边界例外登记和禁止事项的完整清单。
- **Evidence**: 文档明确定义 runtime 在应用启动/销毁、跨 feature 编排、边界例外登记等场景的职责范围和严格限制。

## Impact
- **Scope**: cross-module (影响所有 feature 与 runtime 的交互)
- **Risk**: high (runtime 若过度膨胀将重蹈旧系统全局 store 覆辙)
- **Affected areas**: 所有跨 feature 交互、应用生命周期、平台资源管理

## Relationships
四份文档形成层级约束：
- `rewrite-system-architecture.md` 定义 runtime 使用条件和顶层职责边界
- `rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md` 列出所有需要 runtime orchestration 的交互对
- `rewrite-connection-platform-bridge.md` 定义 connection/platform/runtime 三方职责分界
- `rewrite-thin-ui-runtime-wiring.md` 收敛 runtime 职责的具体 gate 和禁止事项

## Recommendation
- 提取清单后应按职责模块化 runtime 实现：启动 bootstrap、事件路由器、生命周期管理器、边界例外登记器
- 必须在后续 feature design 中显式检查是否需要 runtime 编排，避免默认引入

---

## Runtime 必须履行的职责清单

### 1. 启动/初始化顺序

**必须按以下顺序初始化（从 `rewrite-system-architecture.md` §8 和 `rewrite-thin-ui-runtime-wiring.md` §8）：**

1. **平台资源检查** — 加载 platform bridge health metadata
2. **设置加载** — 从 storage 读取 settings snapshot
3. **Frame 资产加载** — 加载 frame definitions（可选预加载）
4. **Connection adapter 创建** — 创建 real/fake adapter（通过 platform facade）
5. **Feature service 装配** — 按依赖顺序创建各 feature 的 service/reader
6. **事件订阅建立** — 注册跨 feature 的事件路由
7. **应用就绪状态** — 通知 app/router 可以进入主界面

**销毁顺序（反向）：**

1. 停止新任务/操作接收
2. 取消进行中的 task/SCOE 执行
3. 断开 connection transport
4. 刷新 storage 缓冲（如果有）
5. 清理事件订阅
6. 释放 platform 资源
7. 清理 adapter/subscription

### 2. 必须路由的跨 feature 事件

**从 `rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md` §4 中标注为 `runtime orchestration` 或 `explicit event` 的交互：**

#### 高频数据路由（需要节流/批处理/背压）

| 源 feature | 目标 feature | 触发条件 | 传递数据 |
|-----------|-------------|---------|---------|
| **connection** | **receive** | 入站 bytes/batches 到达 | 字节批次 + source material |
| **connection** | **SCOE** | SCOE 固定 source 事件（边界例外） | transport input（bypass generic receive） |
| **receive** | **task** | receive result 匹配 task wait-condition | receive output（显式事件） |
| **receive** | **status** | receive 事实变化 | receive result/delta（节流后） |
| **receive** | **result** | receive 完成（聚合后） | receive output（批量聚合） |
| **receive** | **storage** | 接收数据需要持久化 | receive output（区分正常 vs 高速路径） |
| **send** | **status** | send 结果产生 | send result（条件性高频） |
| **send** | **result** | send 完成（通过 task StepResult） | TaskStepResult（不直接消费 send 事件） |
| **send** | **storage** | send 结果需要记录 | send output（条件性） |
| **send** | **SCOE** | send 结果需记录到 SCOE 工具 | send output（边界例外） |

#### 生命周期事件路由

| 源 feature | 目标 feature | 触发条件 | 传递数据 |
|-----------|-------------|---------|---------|
| **task** | **result** | task 完成（只消费 TaskInstanceCompletion） | TaskInstanceCompletion |
| **task** | **report** | task 完成触发报告生成 | task lifecycle facts |
| **task** | **status** | task 状态变化 | task snapshot |
| **task** | **northbound** | task 外部投影 | task projection |
| **SCOE** | **status** | SCOE 状态变化 | SCOE snapshot |
| **SCOE** | **result** | SCOE 命令完成（通过 task） | TaskInstanceCompletion（不直接消费 SCOE 事件） |
| **result** | **report** | result 就绪 | result snapshot |
| **result** | **northbound** | result 外部投影 | result projection |
| **report** | **storage** | report 文件准备完成 | report file candidate |
| **report** | **northbound** | report 交付准备 | report candidate |
| **northbound** | **task** | 外部命令到达 | validated internal command input |
| **northbound** | **status** | 外部查询/心跳 | external query context |

#### 设置/存储变更路由

| 源 feature | 目标 feature | 触发条件 | 传递数据 |
|-----------|-------------|---------|---------|
| **settings** | **frame** | 启动时或设置变更 | settings snapshot/defaults |
| **settings** | **connection** | 连接配置变更 | config snapshot |
| **settings** | **receive** | 解析配置变更 | config snapshot |
| **settings** | **send** | 发送默认值变更 | config snapshot |
| **settings** | **storage** | 存储路径/策略变更 | config snapshot |
| **settings** | **status** | 指示器配置变更 | config snapshot |
| **settings** | **display** | 显示偏好变更 | config snapshot |
| **storage** | **frame** | 资产导入请求 | import/migration input |
| **storage** | **settings** | 启动时恢复 | persisted snapshot |
| **storage** | **report** | 报告材料请求 | materials |

### 3. 必须管理的生命周期

**从 `rewrite-system-architecture.md` §8 和 `rewrite-connection-platform-bridge.md` §11：**

- **应用启动/关闭** — 统一初始化顺序和清理顺序
- **Platform 资源生命周期** — serial port、TCP socket、UDP socket 的打开/关闭/清理
- **Adapter 生命周期** — real/fake adapter 的创建、替换、释放
- **订阅生命周期** — 事件订阅的建立、清理、stale event 处理
- **Connection 生命周期** — connect、disconnect、cleanup、app shutdown
- **Feature service 生命周期** — service 创建、销毁、状态恢复
- **Storage flush 生命周期** — 缓冲刷新、文件关闭、打包态路径清理

### 4. 必须登记的边界例外

**从 `rewrite-thin-ui-runtime-wiring.md` §8 和 `rewrite-connection-platform-bridge.md` §13：**

- **SCOE 固定 source/target** — SCOE 声明固定连接需求，connection 管理生命周期，runtime 登记例外
- **SCOE 入站 bypass receive** — SCOE 入站直接从 connection transport event 到 SCOE adapter，不经过通用 receive
- **SCOE 出站通过 task→send** — SCOE 不直接调用 send，必须翻译为 TaskDefinition 交由 task 执行
- **高速存储 short-circuit** — main 高速存储规则命中后可能短路普通 receive/display/trigger 链，需登记业务含义和退出条件
- **打包态 data path** — 涉及打包后路径、资源加载、native module 行为的例外
- **Main process 缓冲/批处理** — main 保留的平台侧高频处理（batch、queue、throttle、backpressure）需登记验证方式
- **Northbound delivery** — HTTP/FTP 交付、客户侧接收确认的例外

### 5. 明确禁止做的事

**从四份文档的禁止项汇总（`rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md` §5、`rewrite-thin-ui-runtime-wiring.md` §6、`rewrite-system-architecture.md` §12）：**

- **禁止存储 feature 业务状态** — 不保存 feature 内部 mutable domain state
- **禁止实现领域规则** — 不承载 frame validation、settings merge、storage CSV/history、receive parsing、send framing、task lifecycle、SCOE protocol、result attribution、report generation、northbound 语义转换
- **禁止成为全局业务中心** — 不做全局 store、通用 event bus、command bus 或 service locator
- **禁止直接访问平台细节** — 不直接访问 Electron/Node/preload bridge、`ipcRenderer`、`fs`、`path`、`net`、`serialport`
- **禁止绕过 public API** — 不 import feature 内部 `state`、`adapters`、内部 composable、内部 service 实现
- **禁止写入静态资产** — 统计不得写回 frame definition、field definition、表达式、展示配置
- **禁止为"架构感"引入复杂性** — 不强行引入全局 singleton、复杂 DI 容器、万能 event bus
- **禁止让 UI 持有运行主状态** — pages/widgets 不订阅底层逐包事件，不参与高频统计累加
- **禁止让 storage/history/CSV 等同 TestReport 或 northbound delivery** — 本地导出不是交付闭环
- **禁止把 serial/network target 等同 northbound deviceId** — 传输目标与业务设备身份分离

## Next Steps
- 清单已就绪，可用于指导 runtime 实现设计
- 建议按职责模块拆分：bootstrap、event-router、lifecycle-manager、exception-registry
- 后续 feature design 必须显式检查是否需要 runtime 编排，避免默认引入

## Findings

- **Files**: 
  - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/index.ts` — 核心 runtime 定义和工厂函数
  - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/app/rewriteRuntime.ts` — Vue provide/inject 包装
  - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/app/useRewritePlatform.ts` — 平台信息 facade
  - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/app/AppShell.vue` — 应用壳，负责 provide runtime
  - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/pages/HomePage.vue` — 唯一页面，消费 runtime
  - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/runtime/__tests__/rewrite-runtime.spec.ts` — runtime 测试

- **Root cause**: 当前 runtime 只是一个轻量级的读快照聚合层，缺少所有跨 feature 的编排、事件路由和生命周期管理

- **Evidence**: `createRewriteRuntime` 函数只有 43 行，只聚合了 3 个 feature 的只读端口

## Impact

- **Scope**: 单文件（runtime/index.ts）+ 测试文件
- **Risk**: high — 核心数据通路完全缺失
- **Affected areas**: connection→receive、receive→task、task→send、send→connection、settings→各 feature

## Relationships

```
AppShell.vue (provide)
  ↓
rewriteRuntime.ts (inject/provide 包装)
  ↓
runtime/index.ts (createRewriteRuntime)
  ↓ 聚合只读端口
frameReader + settingsService + storageReader
  ↓ 返回快照
HomePage.vue (useRewriteRuntime)
```

## Recommendation

- runtime 需要显式引入 connection、receive、task、send、status、display 的 service 引用
- 需要建立事件订阅/分发机制（connection 事件 → receive，receive 事件 → task，task 事件 → send）
- 需要提供跨 feature 的编排入口（如启动任务、停止任务、切换连接）

## Next Steps

- Ready for planner — 需要先设计 runtime 的跨 feature 编排契约和数据流拓扑

1	import {
2	  createFrameAssetReader,
3	  type FrameAssetReader,
4	} from '@/features/frame';
5	import {
6	  createSettingsService,
7	  type SettingsOperationResult,
8	  type SettingsResetScope,
9	  type SettingsService,
10	} from '@/features/settings';
11	import {
12	  createStorageLocalReader,
13	  type StorageLocalReader,
14	} from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
15	
16	export interface RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot {
17	  readonly frame: {
18	    readonly totalFrames: number;
19	    readonly totalFields: number;
20	    readonly selectedFrameName: string | null;
21	  };
22	  readonly settings: {
23	    readonly autoStartRecording: boolean;
24	    readonly csvDefaultOutputPath: string;
25	    readonly csvSaveIntervalMinutes: number;
26	  };
27	  readonly storage: {
28	    readonly localRecordCount: number;
29	    readonly historyHourCount: number;
30	    readonly csvMaterialCount: number;
31	    readonly legacyMaterialCount: number;
32	    readonly lastIssue: {
33	      readonly code: string;
34	      readonly message: string;
35	    } | null;
36	  };
37	}
38	
39	export interface RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
40	  readonly ok: boolean;
41	  readonly issues: readonly {
42	    readonly severity: string;
43	    readonly code: string;
44	    readonly message: string;
45	  }[];
46	}
47	
48	type FrameOverviewPort = Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'getSelectedFrame' | 'listFrames'>;
49	type SettingsOverviewPort = Pick<SettingsService, 'getRecordingSettings' | 'reset'>;
50	type StorageOverviewPort = Pick<
51	  StorageLocalReader,
52	  | 'getLastIssue'
53	  | 'listCsvMaterials'
54	  | 'listHistoryHours'
55	  | 'listLegacyMaterials'
56	  | 'listLocalRecords'
57	>;
58	
59	export interface RewriteRuntimeDependencies {
60	  readonly frameReader?: FrameOverviewPort;
61	  readonly settingsService?: SettingsOverviewPort;
62	  readonly storageReader?: StorageOverviewPort;
63	}
64	
65	export interface RewriteRuntime {
66	  getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;
67	  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;
68	}
69	
70	function createDefaultFrameReader(): FrameAssetReader {
71	  return createFrameAssetReader(() => ({
72	    frames: [],
73	  }));
74	}
75	
76	function createDefaultStorageReader(): StorageLocalReader {
77	  return createStorageLocalReader(() => ({
78	    records: [],
79	    historyMaterials: [],
80	    csvMaterials: [],
81	    legacyMaterials: [],
82	  }));
83	}
84	
85	function toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
86	  return {
87	    ok: result.ok,
88	    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({
89	      severity: issue.severity,
90	      code: issue.code,
91	      message: issue.message,
92	    })),
93	  };
94	}
95	
96	export function createRewriteRuntime(
97	  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},
98	): RewriteRuntime {
99	  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? createDefaultFrameReader();
100	  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? createSettingsService();
101	  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? createDefaultStorageReader();
102	
103	  return {
104	    getOverviewSnapshot() {
105	      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();
106	      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();
107	      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();
108	      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();
109	
110	      return {
111	        frame: {
112	          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,
113	          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),
114	          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,
115	        },
116	        settings: {
117	          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,
118	          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,
119	          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,
120	        },
121	        storage: {
122	          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,
123	          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,
124	          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,
125	          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,
126	          lastIssue: storageIssue
127	            ? {
128	                code: storageIssue.code,
129	                message: storageIssue.message,
130	              }
131	            : null,
132	        },
133	      };
134	    },
135	
136	    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {
137	      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));
138	    },
139	  };
140	}
141

1	import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';
2	import { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';
3	
4	const rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');
5	
6	export function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {
7	  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);
8	  return runtime;
9	}
10	
11	export function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {
12	  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);
13	  if (!runtime) {
14	    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');
15	  }
16	
17	  return runtime;
18	}
19

1	import type {
2	  TransportConfig,
3	  TransportErrorKind,
4	  TransportKind,
5	  TransportTargetSnapshot,
6	} from '../core';
7	
8	export type ConnectionAdapterOperation = 'connect' | 'disconnect' | 'write' | 'cleanup';
9	
10	export interface ConnectionAdapterErrorInput {
11	  readonly kind: TransportErrorKind;
12	  readonly message: string;
13	  readonly recoverable?: boolean;
14	}
15	
16	export type ConnectionAdapterEventKind =
17	  | 'connected'
18	  | 'disconnected'
19	  | 'data'
20	  | 'write-accepted'
21	  | 'write-failed'
22	  | 'error';
23	
24	export interface ConnectionAdapterEvent {
25	  readonly kind: ConnectionAdapterEventKind;
26	  readonly connectionId: string;
27	  readonly occurredAt?: string;
28	  readonly target?: TransportTargetSnapshot;
29	  readonly bytes?: readonly number[];
30	  readonly byteLength?: number;
31	  readonly error?: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput;
32	}
33	
34	export interface ConnectionAdapterAccepted {
35	  readonly ok: true;
36	  readonly events: readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[];
37	}
38	
39	export interface ConnectionAdapterRejected {
40	  readonly ok: false;
41	  readonly error: ConnectionAdapterErrorInput;
42	  readonly events?: readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[];
43	}
44	
45	export type ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome =
46	  | ConnectionAdapterAccepted
47	  | ConnectionAdapterRejected;
48	
49	export interface TransportWriteRequest {
50	  readonly connectionId: string;
51	  readonly bytes: readonly number[];
52	  readonly targetId?: string;
53	}
54	
55	export interface ConnectionTransportAdapter {
56	  connect(config: TransportConfig): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>;
57	  disconnect(connectionId: string): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>;
58	  write(request: TransportWriteRequest): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>;
59	  cleanup(): Promise<ConnectionAdapterCommandOutcome>;
60	  drainEvents(): Promise<readonly ConnectionAdapterEvent[]>;
61	}
62	
63	export interface ConnectionResourceCandidate {

65	  readonly id: string;
66	  readonly label: string;
67	}

111	  snapshot: ReceiveStateSnapshot,

163	export function createReceiveService(
164	  options: CreateReceiveServiceOptions = {},
165	): ReceiveService {
166	  const state = options.state ?? createReceiveState();
167	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
168	  const frameReader = options.frameReader ?? createDefaultFrameReader();
169	  const reader = createReceiveReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
170	  let reference: ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot = {
171	    frames: [],
172	    referenceVersion: state.getSnapshot().referenceVersion,
173	    refreshedAt: INITIAL_REFERENCE_REFRESHED_AT,
174	  };
175	
176	  function apply(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
177	    const snapshot = state.applyOutcome(outcome);
178	    return serviceOutcome(snapshot, [outcome]);
179	  }
180	
181	  function refresh(frames: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[]): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
182	    reference = {
183	      frames: cloneFrameReferences(frames),
184	      referenceVersion: reference.referenceVersion + 1,
185	      refreshedAt: now(),
186	    };
187	    const snapshot = state.setReferenceVersion(reference.referenceVersion);
188	    return serviceOutcome(snapshot, [], reference);
189	  }
190	
191	  function ingest(batch: ReceiveInputBatch): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
192	    if (
193	      batch.referenceVersion !== undefined &&
194	      batch.referenceVersion !== reference.referenceVersion
195	    ) {
196	      return apply(
197	        createStaleReceiveInputOutcome(
198	          batch.id,
199	          batch,
200	          reference.referenceVersion,
201	          now(),
202	        ),
203	      );
204	    }
205	
206	    return apply(
207	      processReceiveBatch({
208	        batch,
209	        reference,
210	        processedAt: now(),
211	      }),
212	    );
213	  }
214	
215	  function recordInputError(error: ReceiveInputError): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
216	    return apply(createReceiveInputErrorOutcome(error, now()));
217	  }
218	
219	  return {
220	    ...reader,
221	
222	    refreshFrameReferences(frames) {
223	      return refresh(frames ?? frameReader.findFrames({ direction: 'receive' }));
224	    },
225	
226	    ingestBatch: ingest,
227	
228	    recordInputError,
229	
230	    async drainInputSource(source) {
231	      const outcomes: ReceiveBatchOutcome[] = [];
232	      for (const event of await source.drainEvents()) {
233	        const serviceCall = event.kind === 'batch' ? ingest(event.batch) : recordInputError(event.error);
234	        outcomes.push(...serviceCall.outcomes);
235	      }
236	
237	      return serviceOutcome(state.getSnapshot(), outcomes);
238	    },

---

## 第二步：汇总与规划

### 摸底结论

**当前 runtime 覆盖**：仅聚合 frame/settings/storage-local-baseline 三个 feature 的只读端口，产出 overview snapshot + settings reset。140 行，零事件路由、零生命周期管理、零跨 feature 编排。

**缺失的核心数据通路**（5 条链路全部未接）：

| 链路 | 桥接机制 | 现状 |
|------|---------|------|
| connection → receive | adapter `drainEvents()` 的 data 事件转 `ReceiveInputBatch` → `ingestBatch()` | 完全缺失 |
| receive → task | `ReceiveServiceOutcome.outcomes` 中 `kind==='matched'` 的字段提取为 `ConditionMatchInput` → `ReceiveEventSource.subscribe` | 完全缺失 |
| task → send | `SendServiceProvider.execute()` 直接委托 `sendService.execute()` | 缺桥接适配器 |
| send → connection | `SendTransportWriter.writeBytes()` 委托 `connectionService.write()` | 缺桥接适配器 |
| send target → connection | `SendTargetResolver.resolveTarget()` 委托 `connectionService.listTransportTargets()` | 缺桥接适配器 |

**Feature 依赖顺序**（从 service options 逆向推导）：

```
frame (无外部依赖)
  ↓ 提供 FrameAssetReader
settings (无外部依赖)
  ↓ 提供 SettingsService
connection (依赖 ConnectionTransportAdapter)
  ↓ 提供 write/targets/events
receive (依赖 frameReader.findFrames)
  ↓ 提供 ingestBatch/outcomes
send (依赖 frameReader + connectionBacked: targetResolver + transportWriter)
  ↓ 提供 execute (as SendServiceProvider)
task (依赖 sendService + receiveEventSource bridge)
```

### 模块拆分方案

```
runtime/
  feature-wiring.ts    — 创建所有 feature service + 桥接适配器
  event-router.ts      — connection→receive→task 事件路由
  lifecycle.ts         — 启动/销毁顺序管理
  index.ts             — facade 入口（重构现有）
  __tests__/
    feature-wiring.spec.ts
    event-router.spec.ts
    lifecycle.spec.ts
    rewrite-runtime.spec.ts (更新)
```

### 各模块职责与预估行数

**`feature-wiring.ts`** (~150 行)
- 定义 `RewriteWiredFeatures` — 所有 service/reader 的类型化引用
- 定义 `RewriteWiringOptions` — 可选的 platform adapter 注入（默认 fake）
- 4 个桥接适配器工厂：
  - `createConnectionBackedWriter(connectionService)` → `SendTransportWriter`
  - `createConnectionBackedTargetResolver(connectionService)` → `SendTargetResolver`
  - `createFrameBackedSendReader(frameAssetReader)` → `SendFrameReader`
  - `createReceiveEventSourceBridge()` → `ReceiveEventSource` + `emit()` 方法
- `wireFeatures(options)` — 按依赖顺序创建全部 feature service

**`event-router.ts`** (~120 行)
- `createEventRouter(options)` — 接收 wired features + receive→task bridge
- `tick()` — 单次路由节拍：
  1. `connectionService.drainAdapterEvents()` → 过滤 data 事件
  2. 转 `ReceiveInputBatch` → `receiveService.ingestBatch()`
  3. 检查 outcomes，提取 matched 字段 → `ConditionMatchInput`
  4. 发布到 receiveEventSourceBridge
- `start()` / `stop()` — 启停控制
- `getRoutingStats()` — 路由统计（测试可观测性）

**`lifecycle.ts`** (~80 行)
- `RuntimeLifecycle` 接口
- `createRuntimeLifecycle(options)`:
  - `init()` → wireFeatures + createEventRouter + start
  - `destroy()` → stop router + cleanup connection + 清理订阅
  - `isReady` getter

**`index.ts`** (~100 行，重构现有)
- 保留 `RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot` 和 `getOverviewSnapshot()` 向后兼容
- `createRewriteRuntime()` 内部使用 wiring + lifecycle
- 扩展 `RewriteRuntime` 接口暴露 feature 引用和 tick

### 实施批次

| 批次 | 内容 | 可并行 |
|------|------|--------|
| 1 | `feature-wiring.ts` + 测试 | 基础，不可并行 |
| 2 | `event-router.ts` + 测试 | 依赖批 1 的类型 |
| 3 | `lifecycle.ts` + `index.ts` 重构 + 测试更新 | 依赖批 1+2 |

批 1 和批 2 可由两个 executor 并行（批 2 只依赖批 1 的类型定义，可提前约定接口）。批 3 串行在最后。

---

改动范围足够清晰，架构文档和 feature API 已完全定义了约束。是否直接推进实施，还是走 `cs-feat-design` 产出正式 design doc？

> DEVELOPER

核心问题：tick() 轮询模型是错误的抽象

  方案最关键的问题是 event-router.ts 用 tick() 做轮询。这违反了架构文档的核心约束，也忽略了 feature 已有的 adapter 机制。

  证据一：架构文档明确要求 explicit event

  交互矩阵对 connection→receive 的要求：

  ▎ interaction candidate: explicit event, runtime orchestration, selector
  ▎ High freq: Yes

  对 receive→task：

  ▎ receive result/delta 可能有多消费者，不能同步堆副作用
  ▎ 应优先考虑 explicit event 或 runtime orchestration

  tick() 把 5 个 feature 的数据流同步串在一个函数调用里，本质上是用 runtime 重写了旧 receiveFramesStore 的尾部副作用链——这正是架构文档反复强调要避免的。

  证据二：feature 已经提供了 event-driven 接口，方案没用到

  实际 public API 比方案假设的更完善：

  ┌────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │  Feature   │                                   已有接口                                   │                        方案怎么用的                         │
  ├────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ connection │ drainAdapterEvents(): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome> 包含 events[]      │ ✅ 方案注意到了                                             │
  ├────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ receive    │ drainInputSource(source: ReceiveInputSource): Promise<ReceiveServiceOutcome> │ ❌ 方案没看到，自己手动调 ingestBatch() 做转换              │
  ├────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ receive    │ ReceiveInputSource adapter 接口 drainEvents(): Promise<ReceiveInputEvent[]>  │ ❌ 方案自己手动做 connection event → ReceiveInputBatch 转换 │
  ├────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ task       │ ReceiveEventSource.subscribe(handler) — 回调注册模式                         │ ⚠️ 方案用了 bridge 但在 tick() 里同步触发                   │
  └────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  关键发现：receive 已经设计了 ReceiveInputSource adapter 接口和 drainInputSource() 方法。正确做法是：

  runtime 创建 ConnectionToReceiveInputSource adapter
    → 包装 connection adapter events 为 ReceiveInputEvent[]
    → 调用 receiveService.drainInputSource(adapter)
    → 检查 outcomes，bridge 到 task 的 ReceiveEventSource

  这比 tick() 里手动 ingestBatch + 手动提取 matched fields 简单得多，而且复用了 receive 已有的批处理逻辑。

  证据三：同步 tick 链不适合高频数据

  串口 115200 baud ≈ 11KB/s，TCP 可能更高。tick() 如果不能在一个 tick 内处理完所有待处理数据，就会积压；如果能，那就是在一个同步调用里处理大量数据 + 触发 task + 触发 send，和旧系统一次
  receiveFramesStore.handleReceivedData() 完成所有副作用没有区别。

  ---
  其他问题

  1. ConnectionService.write() 的签名对不上

  方案说 SendTransportWriter.writeBytes() 委托 connectionService.write()。实际：

  // connection public API
  write(request: TransportWriteRequest): Promise<ConnectionOperationOutcome>;

  // send adapter port
  writeBytes(connectionId: string, bytes: readonly number[]): Promise<SendTransportWriteOutcome>;

  两者签名不同，bridge adapter 需要正确转换，方案没提 TransportWriteRequest 的结构。

  2. storage-local-baseline 被丢弃

  当前 runtime/index.ts 已经接了 storage-local-baseline（overview 里的 storage 字段），方案的 5 条链路和模块设计完全没提它。重构后会丢失现有功能。

  3. settings 消费机制缺失

  方案在依赖顺序里列了 settings，但 wiring 和 event-router 都没展示 settings snapshot 如何分发到消费 feature。架构文档说 settings → 各 feature 用 selector 或 runtime orchestration，但方案没设计这个。

  4. wireFeatures() 太粗

  一个函数创建 6 个 service + 4 个 bridge adapter，任何一个失败全部失败。应该按依赖层级逐步创建，每步返回结果。

  ---
  什么是对的

  - 5 条数据通路的识别正确
  - 依赖顺序 frame→settings→connection→receive→send→task 正确
  - 模块拆分（wiring / router / lifecycle / index）方向合理
  - bridge adapter 的思路正确（runtime 在 feature 公开接口之间做桥接）
  - 实施批次拆分合理

  ---
  建议

  把你的分析反馈给那个对话，核心修改：

  1. 删掉 tick()，改为 event-driven 模型：
    - runtime 创建 ConnectionToReceiveInputSource adapter（实现 ReceiveInputSource）
    - connection adapter 有事件时，runtime 调用 receiveService.drainInputSource(adapter)
    - 检查 outcomes → bridge 到 task 的 ReceiveEventSource
  2. 补 storage-local-baseline 的保留方案
  3. 补 settings 分发机制（至少在 wiring 时注入 settings snapshot 到需要的 feature）
  4. wireFeatures 按 dependency level 拆分，不是一个大函数 你在干什么？

> AGENT

设计错误，接受全部修正。直接出修订方案。

核心修正点：
1. 删 tick()，改 event-driven：利用 `ReceiveInputSource` adapter 接口
2. 保留 storage-local-baseline
3. 补 settings 分发
4. wireFeatures 按依赖层级拆分
5. 修正 write bridge 的签名转换

---

## 修订方案

### 事件驱动模型

关键变更：runtime 不做轮询，而是创建桥接 adapter 把 connection 事件对接到 receive 已有的 `ReceiveInputSource` 接口。

```
ConnectionAdapter.drainEvents()
       ↓
ConnectionToReceiveInputSource (implements ReceiveInputSource)
  .drainEvents() → 把 ConnectionAdapterEvent.data 转为 ReceiveInputEvent[]
       ↓
receiveService.drainInputSource(source)
       ↓
ReceiveServiceOutcome.outcomes
  过滤 kind==='matched'，提取字段 → ConditionMatchInput[]
       ↓
ReceiveEventSourceBridge.emit(inputs)
  → task 已注册的 subscribe handler 收到回调
```

这完全复用了 receive 的批处理逻辑，runtime 不手动调 `ingestBatch()`，不手动提取 matched fields。

### feature-wiring.ts 修订 — 按层级创建

```ts
// Level 0: 无外部依赖
interface WiringLevel0 {
  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
  readonly settingsService: SettingsService;
  readonly storageReader: StorageLocalReader;  // 保留现有
}

// Level 1: 依赖 adapter
interface WiringLevel1 extends WiringLevel0 {
  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
}

// Level 2: 依赖 frame
interface WiringLevel2 extends WiringLevel1 {
  readonly receiveService: ReceiveService;
}

// Level 3: 依赖 frame + connection
interface WiringLevel3 extends WiringLevel2 {
  readonly sendService: SendService;
}

// Level 4: 依赖 send + receive bridge
interface WiringLevel4 extends WiringLevel3 {
  readonly taskService: TaskService;
  readonly receiveEventSourceBridge: ReceiveEventSourceBridge;
}

function wireLevel0(options?): WiringLevel0;
function wireLevel1(adapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter, prev: WiringLevel0): WiringLevel1;
function wireLevel2(prev: WiringLevel1): WiringLevel2;
function wireLevel3(prev: WiringLevel2): WiringLevel3;
function wireLevel4(prev: WiringLevel3): WiringLevel4;
```

每个 level 是独立函数，失败只影响本层。测试可以只测某一层的 wiring。

### 桥接适配器

**1. ConnectionToReceiveInputSource** (implements `ReceiveInputSource`)
```ts
// 包装 connection adapter 为 receive 的 ReceiveInputSource
class ConnectionToReceiveInputSource implements ReceiveInputSource {
  constructor(private connectionAdapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter) {}
  
  async drainEvents(): Promise<ReceiveInputEvent[]> {
    const outcome = await this.connectionAdapter.drainEvents();
    // ConnectionAdapterEvent.data → ReceiveInputEvent.batch
  }
}
```

**2. ConnectionBackedSendWriter** (implements `SendTransportWriter`)
```ts
// SendTransportWriter.writeBytes(cid, bytes) → ConnectionService.write({connectionId, bytes})
// 注意 TransportWriteRequest 结构: {connectionId, bytes, targetId?}
// 返回值转换: ConnectionOperationOutcome → SendTransportWriteOutcome
```

**3. ConnectionBackedTargetResolver** (implements `SendTargetResolver`)
```ts
// SendTargetResolver.resolveTarget(id) → ConnectionService.listTransportTargets()
// TransportTargetSnapshot 类型两边一致，直接委托
```

**4. ReceiveEventSourceBridge** (implements `ReceiveEventSource`)
```ts
// 简单的 pub/sub bridge
// subscribe(handler) → 注册回调
// emit(inputs: ConditionMatchInput[]) → 通知所有 handler
// clear() → 清理订阅
```

### event-router.ts 修订 — event-driven

```ts
interface EventRouter {
  start(): void;     // 开始路由
  stop(): void;      // 停止路由
  isRunning(): boolean;
}

function createEventRouter(wiring: WiringLevel4, options?: {
  pollIntervalMs?: number;  // 仅用于驱动 drainAdapterEvents 的调度
}): EventRouter;
```

路由节拍（由调度器驱动，不是同步 tick）：
1. 调用 `connectionToReceiveInputSource`（内部 drain connection adapter）
2. 调用 `receiveService.drainInputSource(connToReceiveAdapter)`
3. 从 `outcomes` 中提取 matched → `ConditionMatchInput[]`
4. `receiveEventSourceBridge.emit(inputs)`

与 tick() 的本质区别：
- 不手动调 `ingestBatch()`，用 `drainInputSource` 复用 receive 批处理
- 不手动提取 matched fields，从 `outcomes` 中取结构化结果
- 不串 task+send，task 通过 `ReceiveEventSource.subscribe` 异步回调
- 调度只负责驱动 drain，不做业务逻辑

### settings 分发

在 wiring 层处理，不在 event-router 里：
- `wireLevel0` 创建 `settingsService`
- `wireLevel2` 创建 receive 时，settings 已在 level0 中可访问
- 后续需要运行时分发时，在 event-router 中加 settings change → 各 feature 的 routeSettingsChange()

当前阶段 settings 主要通过各 feature 构造时注入或 selector 读取，暂不需要运行时推送。

### index.ts 重构

保留现有 `RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot`、`getOverviewSnapshot()`、`resetSettings()` 向后兼容。扩展 `RewriteRuntime` 接口：

```ts
interface RewriteRuntime {
  // 现有
  getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;
  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;
  
  // 新增
  readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;  // Level4 完整引用
  readonly lifecycle: RuntimeLifecycle;
}
```

### 实施批次（修订）

| 批次 | 内容 | 依赖 |
|------|------|------|
| 1 | `feature-wiring.ts` — 5 层 wiring + 4 个 bridge adapter + 测试 | 无 |
| 2a | `event-router.ts` — event-driven 路由 + 测试 | 批 1 类型 |
| 2b | `lifecycle.ts` — 启动/销毁 + 测试 | 批 1 类型 |
| 3 | `index.ts` 重构 + 现有测试更新 + build/lint | 批 1+2 |

批 2a 和 2b 可并行。

---

方案已修订。推进实施？

> DEVELOPER

你觉得，它存不存在什么，设计过于复杂、功能边界不清、代码重复、设计不满足需求、后续代码无法维护等问题？

● 有实质性问题，一个会导致运行时数据丢失。

  ---
  问题 1（会出 bug）：ConnectionToReceiveInputSource 绕过了 ConnectionService

  方案设计的流程：

  ConnectionAdapter.drainEvents()       ← 桥接 adapter 直接调底层 adapter
         ↓
  ConnectionToReceiveInputSource

  但 ConnectionService 已经持有 ConnectionTransportAdapter。如果桥接 adapter 也调 adapter.drainEvents()，会出现：

  1. ConnectionService 和桥接 adapter 竞争同一个 adapter 的事件队列
  2. ConnectionService 的 state 不会更新——它的 counters、event history、lastError 全部丢失
  3. 串口/TCP 断线、连接状态变化等非 data 事件也被桥接 adapter 吃掉，ConnectionService 看不到

  正确流程必须先经过 ConnectionService：

  event-router 调用 connectionService.drainAdapterEvents()
    → ConnectionService 内部 drain adapter，更新自身 state，返回 ConnectionOperationOutcome
    → outcome.events 里有 data 事件
         ↓
  event-router 把 data 事件推入 ConnectionToReceiveInputSource
    → 桥接 adapter 缓存这些事件
         ↓
  receiveService.drainInputSource(bridge)
    → receive 内部调 bridge.drainEvents()，拿到 ReceiveInputEvent[]

  ConnectionToReceiveInputSource 不是包装 ConnectionTransportAdapter，而是接收 ConnectionService 已经处理过的事件，做格式转换。它应该是无状态的事件转换器，不是 adapter 的代理。

  ---
  问题 2（过度设计）：5 层 wiring 拆得太细

  6 个 service 5 个 level，其中 4 个 level 各自只创建 1 个 service。维护 5 个函数 + 5 个接口的成本高于收益。

  实际依赖关系：

  Level 0 (无 feature 依赖): frame, settings, storage
  Level 1 (需 adapter):      connection
  Level 2 (需 level 0+1):    receive, send
  Level 3 (需 level 2):      task + bridge

  3 层足够，每层内的 service 互相独立可以并行创建。Level 2 的 receive 和 send 可以同时创建（receive 依赖 frame，send 依赖 frame+connection，都在前置层已有）。

  ---
  问题 3（缺失）：错误处理完全没提

  event-router 的路由节拍中任何一个环节都可能失败：

  - connectionService.drainAdapterEvents() 返回 error outcome
  - receiveService.drainInputSource() 返回 issues
  - bridge emit 时 task handler 抛异常

  方案没有设计：
  - 单步失败后是否继续后续步骤
  - 错误如何暴露给上层（lifecycle / UI）
  - 是否需要重试 / 退避 / 断路

  不需要复杂方案，但至少需要：路由节拍中每步检查 outcome.ok，失败时记录并跳过后续步骤，不吞错误。

  ---
  问题 4（表述不诚实）：pollIntervalMs 说明不是纯 event-driven

  方案标题写"事件驱动模型"，但 event-router 仍然用 pollIntervalMs 做轮询调度。这不是真正的 event-driven（adapter 不主动 push），是轮询驱动的批处理。

  这个设计本身没有错——桌面应用轮询串口/TCP adapter 是合理的抽象层级。真正的 event-driven 需要平台层提供 callback/push 能力，那是 platform facade 的事，不是 runtime 的事。但标题应该诚实，避免后续实现者误解。

  ---
  没问题的部分

  - 4 个桥接 adapter 的职责划分正确
  - settings 在 wiring 时注入、暂不需要运行时推送判断正确
  - ReceiveEventSourceBridge 作为简单 pub/sub bridge 合理
  - index.ts 向后兼容扩展 合理
  - 实施批次拆分（1 → 2a+2b 并行 → 3）合理

  ---
  总结

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────┬──────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                问题                 │  严重度  │              修复成本               │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 桥接 adapter 绕过 ConnectionService │ 会出 bug │ 改桥接 adapter 的构造参数和数据来源 │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 5 层 wiring 过细                    │ 中       │ 合并为 3 层                         │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 缺错误处理                          │ 中       │ 路由节拍加 outcome 检查             │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ poll vs event-driven 表述           │ 低       │ 改措辞                              │
  └─────────────────────────────────────┴──────────┴─────────────────────────────────────┘

  把这四点反馈回去，特别是问题 1——不改的话运行时 connection 的 state 会是空的。

> AGENT

四个问题全部成立，逐条采纳。

**修正 1**：桥接 adapter 不持有 `ConnectionTransportAdapter`，改为接收 `ConnectionService.drainAdapterEvents()` 返回的已处理事件，做纯格式转换。`ConnectionToReceiveInputSource` 是无状态转换器。

**修正 2**：5 层合并为 3 层 + 桥接层：

```
Level 0: frame, settings, storage-local-baseline  (并行创建，无互依赖)
Level 1: connection                                  (需 adapter)
Level 2: receive, send                               (并行创建，需 L0+L1)
Level 3: task + receiveEventSourceBridge             (需 L2)
```

**修正 3**：路由节拍每步检查 outcome，失败记录并跳过后续。

**修正 4**：措辞改为"轮询驱动的批处理路由"，不用"event-driven"。

14	
15	# Rewrite thin UI / runtime wiring
16	
17	## 1. Direct contract
18	
19	本轮依据以下正式工件判断范围和完成度：
20	
21	1. `AGENTS.md`
22	2. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`
23	3. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md`
24	4. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-boundaries.md`
25	5. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-feature-interaction-matrix.md`
26	6. `codestable/architecture/rewrite-platform-app-shell-file-dialog.md`
27	7. `codestable/quality/rewrite-validation-fixture-oracle-baseline.md`
28	8. `codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
29	9. `codestable/quality/rewrite-review-checklist.md`
30	10. `codestable/features/rewrite-frame/rewrite-frame-design.md`
31	11. `codestable/features/rewrite-settings/rewrite-settings-design.md`
32	12. `codestable/features/rewrite-storage-local-baseline/rewrite-storage-local-baseline-design.md`
33	13. 当前实现参考：
34	    - `rewrite/src/features/frame`
35	    - `rewrite/src/features/settings`
36	    - `rewrite/src/features/storage-local-baseline`
37	    - `rewrite/src/app`
38	    - `rewrite/src/pages`
39	    - `rewrite/src/widgets`
40	    - `rewrite/src/runtime`
41	
42	`codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md` 仍是目录、依赖方向、feature public API 和 runtime 职责的 canonical 架构基线。本文只收窄 app/pages/widgets/runtime 如何消费 feature public API，不替代 frame/settings/storage 的 feature design，也不定义新的平台 API schema。
43	
44	## 2. Boundary guards
45	
46	- 本轮只做 design，不实现 UI，不实现 runtime wiring。
47	- 不决定视觉细节、布局密度、组件样式或具体页面交互文案。
48	- 不接 platform、preload、main，不定义 bridge/channel/method/payload/error schema。
49	- 不进入 receive、send、task、SCOE、result、report、northbound 的内部设计。
50	- 不引入 Pinia 或任何 global store 作为业务全局中心。
51	- 不让 runtime 保存 feature 内部业务 state。
52	- 不让 pages/widgets import feature internal state、internal service、adapter、internal composable 或私有 helper。
53	- 不让 UI 持有运行主状态、任务生命周期、协议语义、统计累加或文件/串口/网络访问。
54	- 不把 storage/history/CSV/local export 等同 report、TestReport、HTTP/FTP 或 northbound delivery closure。
55	
56	## 3. Current evidence summary
57	
58	当前 `rewrite` 已经形成最小应用壳和三个小 feature public surface：
59	
60	| Area | Current fact | Design implication |
61	| --- | --- | --- |
62	| `app/pages` | `rewrite/src/App.vue` 只承载 router view；`rewrite/src/app/AppShell.vue` 只做 Quasar shell + router outlet；`rewrite/src/pages/HomePage.vue` 只展示 bridge info。 | app/pages 仍足够薄，后续应保持“路由入口 + 用户动作连接”，不要把 feature 装配和业务流程塞进页面。 |
63	| `widgets/runtime` | `rewrite/src/widgets` 和 `rewrite/src/runtime` 当前只有 `.gitkeep`。 | 这两个目录尚未形成事实实现；本文只能定义 gate 和消费规则，不能声称 runtime wiring 已完成。 |
64	| `platform` | `rewrite/src/platform/index.ts` 只读 bridge metadata；preload/main 仍是 metadata-only baseline。 | 本轮不扩大平台能力；UI/runtime 只能按未来 facade 类别消费，不直接接 Electron/Node。 |
65	| feature public API | `frame/settings/storage-local-baseline` 根 `index.ts` 只导出 reader/service/selectors/types/constants 等 public surface；测试已断言 root API 不导出 `create*State`、fake adapter 等内部对象。 | 后续 pages/runtime 只从 feature root import，禁止从 feature 子目录绕过 public surface。 |
66	| feature state | 当前 service 通过 feature 自己的 state container 写入本域状态，并返回 `ok / validation / snapshot` 或 read model。selectors 返回 clone 或 projection。 | 适合作为 first wiring 的消费模型：读写分离、只读 snapshot、显式操作结果。 |
67	| tests | Vitest 当前覆盖 frame/settings/storage 的 core/service/state/selector/fake adapter；页面交互没有自动 UI 测试 scaffold。 | UI wiring 只能先规划 manual checklist；静态检查和 feature 单测不能替代 runtime、package、hardware、customer 证据。 |
68	
69	旧系统入口事实只作为入口保留证据：旧 `src/router/routes.ts` 和 `src/components/layout/SidePanel.vue` 暴露 `/`、`/connect`、`/frames/list`、`/frames/editor`、`/frames/send`、`/frames/receive`、`/settings`、`/storage`、`/history`、`/scoe` 等入口。本文只把其中与 frame/settings/storage local baseline 相关的入口列为 first wiring candidates，其他入口 deferred。
70	
71	## 4. Responsibilities
72	
73	| Layer | Owns | Can call | Must not own |
74	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
75	| `app/` | 应用启动入口、router/provider 安装、应用壳、全局 layout slot、最低限度的生命周期挂载点。 | router、Pinia 安装工厂、runtime bootstrap 候选、platform health metadata 候选、widgets shell 组件。 | 领域规则、feature state、服务内部实现、平台资源细节、页面展示态、任务/协议/报告语义。 |
76	| `pages/` | 路由页面、页面级布局、把用户操作连接到 runtime page API 或 feature public API、页面本地 UI snapshot。 | feature root public API、feature page composable、runtime page API、widgets props/events。 | feature internal state、跨 feature 业务流程、平台直连、协议解析、任务推进、统计累加、文件/串口/网络能力。 |
77	| `widgets/` | 跨页面纯展示组件、通用控件、导航壳、状态/摘要的无业务 owner 展示。 | `shared` 纯类型/工具、props、emits、由 page/runtime 传入的只读 snapshot。仅在 feature design 明确声明时读取 widget-safe public selector。 | feature internal state、feature service 操作、运行主状态、platform facade、文件对话框语义、任务/结果/报告/northbound 语义。 |
78	| `features/<feature>/components` | 只服务本 feature 的局部 UI，例如 frame list/editor 内部控件、settings 表单分组、storage/history 局部控件。 | 本 feature composable、root public API、props/emits、本 feature read model。 | 其他 feature internal、runtime 编排、平台直连、跨 feature 业务流程。 |
79	| `features/<feature>/composables` | UI-facing 组合，管理表单草稿、筛选、分页、dialog state、selector 组合和调用本 feature public service。 | 本 feature root/internal 按 feature 内部规则访问、必要的 page-local inputs。 | 协议语义、任务状态机、统计累加、跨 feature 写状态、platform/main API。 |
80	| `runtime/` | 应用级组合根、启动/销毁顺序、feature service/adapter/context 装配、跨 feature public API 编排、边界例外登记。 | feature root public API、platform facade adapter factory、显式事件/command 候选、app lifecycle hooks。 | feature 内部业务 state、领域算法、UI snapshot、main/preload schema、receive/send/task/SCOE/result/report/northbound 语义。 |
81	
82	Thin UI 的默认路径是：
83	
84	```text
85	page user action
86	  -> feature page composable or runtime page API
87	  -> feature root public service / reader / selector
88	  -> feature-owned state/core/adapter
89	  -> readonly read model or operation result
90	  -> page-local UI snapshot
91	  -> widget/feature component props
92	```
93	
94	## 5. Allowed imports
95	
96	Allowed imports are intentionally narrow:
97	
98	| Importing layer | Allowed imports |
99	| --- | --- |
100	| `app/` | `vue` / `vue-router` / Quasar boot primitives, `@/router`, `@/stores`, `@/runtime` bootstrap candidates, `@/platform` health metadata, app-owned widgets. |
101	| `pages/` | `@/features/<feature>` root public API, `@/features/<feature>/composables` only for the same feature page after that composable is declared UI-facing, `@/runtime` page API, `@/widgets`, `@/shared` pure types/helpers. |
102	| `widgets/` | `@/shared`, framework UI primitives, props/emits types, explicitly passed read-only snapshots. A direct feature root import is exceptional and must be declared widget-safe in the owning feature design. |
103	| `features/<feature>/components` | Same feature composables/selectors/types, `@/shared`, pure widgets. |
104	| `features/<feature>/composables` | Same feature public service/selector/state adapter through feature-owned paths, page-local UI inputs, `@/shared`. |
105	| `runtime/` | `@/features/<feature>` root public API, `@/platform` facade, `@/shared`, explicit event/command utilities if later justified. |
106	
107	Pinia can remain an application provider or a feature-local state implementation detail, but it is not a cross-feature service locator. Stores must not create services, store platform adapters, or expose Electron/Node capabilities.
108	
109	## 6. Forbidden imports
110	
111	Forbidden imports:
112	
113	- `pages` / `widgets` / `app` / `runtime` importing `@/features/<feature>/state`.
114	- `pages` / `widgets` importing `@/features/<feature>/adapters`, fake adapters, or adapter ports.
115	- `pages` / `widgets` importing `@/features/<feature>/services/<file>` or `selectors/<file>` to bypass root public API.
116	- Any UI layer importing `fs`、`path`、`net`、`serialport`、Electron、`ipcRenderer`、preload bridge details or old `window.electron`.
117	- `widgets` importing multiple feature internals and constructing business summaries itself.
118	- `runtime` importing feature internal state containers to keep or mutate feature truth.
119	- Any layer outside a feature importing another feature's private helper, internal composable or internal service implementation.
120	- `shared` importing Vue、Pinia、Electron、platform facade or feature-owned business helpers.
121	- Auto-importing feature service、platform facade、runtime、store、feature public API、adapter or business-owned helper.
122	
123	Static checks for later implementation should include `rg` patterns for feature subpath imports from `rewrite/src/app rewrite/src/pages rewrite/src/widgets rewrite/src/runtime`, and for renderer direct Node/Electron imports.
124	
125	## 7. Feature public API consumption rules
126	
127	Public API consumption rules:
128	
129	1. The feature root `rewrite/src/features/<feature>/index.ts` is the default external import boundary.
130	2. Root exports should stay capability-oriented: reader, service, selector, validation result, read-only snapshot, constants and stable public types.
131	3. External consumers may call `create*Reader` for query-only flows and `create*Service` for mutation flows. They must not hold or mutate feature state containers.
132	4. Mutation results should be handled as explicit operation results, for example `ok / validation / snapshot / error`. UI should derive display state from the returned read model, not from internal state references.
133	5. Read models and snapshots are inputs for display and orchestration. They are not permission to write feature truth.
134	6. Runtime may create services and adapters when lifecycle, test replacement, platform resource setup, or cross-feature orchestration requires it. Pages should not create adapter-backed services for file/path/dialog/storage behavior.
135	7. Same-feature pages may use a feature-owned UI composable once that composable is explicitly scoped to UI projection and service calls. Cross-feature pages should use runtime page API or compose read-only snapshots at page level.
136	8. Widgets should receive already-shaped props or UI snapshots. They should not call write services.
137	9. If a feature needs to expose a new external operation, update that feature design/checklist before wiring pages/runtime to it.
138	10. If a consumer needs an internal subpath import to finish a page, that is a design smell: either promote a narrow public API or move the UI into the owning feature component/composable boundary.
139	
140	Current first public surfaces:
141	
142	| Feature | Public surface already visible | Thin consumption stance |
143	| --- | --- | --- |
144	| `frame` | `createFrameAssetReader`、`createFrameAssetService`、frame selectors/types/constants. | Pages may read lists/details/reference options and call service operations through feature page composables; runtime may preload or provide a frame reader to consumers. No receive/send/task/SCOE runtime facts enter frame. |
145	| `settings` | `createSettingsReader`、`createSettingsService`、recording/CSV selectors/types. | Runtime may load a settings snapshot before initializing consumers; settings page may update/reset via service. Consumers receive explicit snapshots, not settings internal state. |
146	| `storage-local-baseline` | `createStorageLocalReader`、`createStorageLocalService`、local material selectors/types; service requires adapter options. | Runtime or feature adapter layer owns adapter-backed service creation. Pages only consume storage summaries/material projections and operation results. |
147	
148	## 8. Runtime involvement gate
149	
150	Before adding any `runtime/` file or API, answer these gates in the design or checklist:
151	
152	| Gate | Runtime may participate when yes | Runtime must stay out when no |
153	| --- | --- | --- |
154	| Lifecycle gate | Startup, shutdown, flush, restore, cleanup or resource release order spans multiple services or adapters. | A single feature page can call its own public service without cross-feature sequencing. |
155	| Cross-feature gate | One user action must call multiple feature public APIs in a defined order, or route explicit outputs from one owner to another. | The flow belongs entirely to one feature's service/composable. |
156	| Adapter gate | Platform-backed adapters need centralized construction, fake replacement, disposal or environment registration. | The service is pure and has no lifecycle or platform-backed dependency. |
157	| Event gate | Multiple consumers need an async notification from a feature output, with owner/producer/consumer/failure semantics declared. | A direct service call or selector read is enough. |
158	| Validation gate | Runtime/package/hardware/customer gaps must be recorded because the flow cannot be proven by Vitest/fixture/manual alone. | The change is pure feature core/service/selector or pure UI snapshot. |
159	| Exception gate | SCOE fixed source/target, high-speed storage short-circuit, packaged data path, HTTP/FTP or similar exception is involved. | No boundary exception exists. |
160	
161	Runtime allowed responsibilities:
162	
163	- Create feature services, adapters and context from explicit factories.
164	- Load settings/frame/storage snapshots in a documented startup order.
165	- Route page-level commands to feature public services.
166	- Coordinate read-only snapshots across features for a page or widget.
167	- Register boundary exceptions and validation gaps.
168	- Provide teardown/flush hooks for storage or future platform-backed adapters.
169	
170	Runtime forbidden responsibilities:
171	
172	- Store feature business truth or mutable domain state.
173	- Implement frame validation, settings merge rules, storage CSV/history semantics, receive parsing, send framing, task lifecycle, SCOE protocol, result attribution, report generation or northbound external semantics.
174	- Become a global store, universal event bus, command bus by default, or service locator.
175	- Access Electron/Node/preload bridge details directly.
176	
177	## 9. UI snapshot and feature state separation
178	
179	Use three named layers for UI wiring:
180	
181	| Layer | Owner | Write rule | Typical examples |
182	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
183	| Feature core state | Owning feature state/service. | Only owning feature service/state writes it. | frame asset collection, settings snapshot, storage local records/materials. |
184	| Feature read model | Owning feature selectors/readers. | Derived from feature core state or explicit feature outputs; read-only for consumers. | frame summaries, field reference options, CSV export preference, storage hour summaries. |
185	| UI snapshot | Page, feature UI composable, or widget boundary. | UI writes only display concerns; never writes back as feature truth. | search text, filters, pagination, selected tab, dialog draft, loading state, button disabled state, empty/error presentation. |
186	
187	Rules:
188	
189	- UI snapshot can shape data for display, including sorting, filtering, pagination, virtual scrolling, throttling and temporary errors.
190	- UI snapshot cannot define whether a task succeeded, whether a device is connected, what a received value means, whether a report is valid, or whether delivery succeeded.
191	- Feature service returns are converted into read model or UI snapshot at the page/composable boundary.
192	- High-frequency data must enter pages only as throttled read model or UI snapshot. Pages/widgets must not subscribe to bottom-level packet events.
193	- If a widget needs data from multiple features, page/runtime composes a read-only view model first and passes props to the widget.
194	- UI snapshot may be persisted only after the owning feature design explicitly upgrades it to a settings/storage-owned configuration fact.
195	
196	## 10. Testing and manual checklist plan
197	
198	This design only adds documentation. Verification for this round is static scan only.
199	
200	Future implementation should split evidence by validation level:
201	
202	| Layer | Evidence target | Scope |
203	| --- | --- | --- |
204	| Static scan | Import boundaries, section presence, forbidden direct Node/Electron imports. | `rg` for docs and imports. |
205	| Vitest unit | Feature core/service/selector/fake adapter behavior. | Existing frame/settings/storage tests and new tests under owning feature. |
206	| Fixture/oracle | Legacy JSON, settings key normalization, history/CSV expected output, operation result edge cases. | Feature-owned `fixtures/` or `oracles/`; not production schema. |
207	| Manual checklist | Route reachability, navigation, visible page state, form draft, validation display, dialog flow, empty/error state. | Pages/widgets/app shell only; cannot prove core/platform/hardware correctness. |
208	| Runtime validation | Startup order, runtime bootstrap, adapter creation/disposal, settings change propagation, storage flush. | Development Electron runtime once implementation exists. |
209	| Package validation | Packaged data path, permissions, long-running persistence, cleanup. | Storage/app packaging later. |
210	| Hardware/customer validation | Real serial/TCP/UDP/SCOE, high-speed throughput, HTTP/FTP/northbound/customer closure. | Deferred; not claimed by thin UI wiring. |
211	
212	Manual checklist template for first wiring candidates:
213	
214	| Candidate | Manual checks | Automatic checks |
215	| --- | --- | --- |
216	| App shell + navigation | Route entries visible and reachable; active route state; unknown route reaches 404; no business state shown as global truth. | Static import scan. |
217	| Frame list/editor page shell | List/detail/editor entry reachable; page calls feature public API/composable only; validation results displayed from service output. | Existing frame service/selector tests plus import scan. |
218	| Settings page shell | Values load from settings snapshot; edits call settings service; reset scope follows settings design; no runtime facts stored in settings. | Existing settings core/service/selector tests plus import scan. |
219	| Storage/history local page shell | History/CSV/material summaries come from storage read model; adapter-backed operations are routed through runtime/feature service, not widgets. | Existing storage service/fake adapter tests plus import scan. |
220	
221	Implementation completion claims must not use page visibility, build, lint or fixture tests to claim Electron runtime, package data path, hardware, HTTP/FTP, northbound or customer validation complete.
222	
223	## 11. First wiring candidates
224	
225	First candidates should be limited to app/pages/widgets/runtime edges that can consume the already existing frame/settings/storage-local-baseline public APIs without entering deferred domains:
226	
227	| Candidate | Scope | Allowed data/API | Runtime need |
228	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
229	| App shell route map | Rebuild visible route container and navigation model from old entry evidence for `/`、`/frames/list`、`/frames/editor`、`/settings`、`/storage`、`/history` plus catch-all. | Static route metadata and widget props. | Not required unless route guards or startup readiness become real. |
230	| Navigation widget | Cross-page navigation display/control only. | Route metadata props and current route state. | Not required. |
231	| Home page | Baseline landing/status placeholder. | Platform bridge metadata only while platform remains metadata-only. | Not required. |
232	| Frame list/editor page shells | Connect frame page actions to `frame` root public reader/service via feature UI composable. | Frame summaries, details, validation result, selected frame snapshot. | Optional startup preload only; no cross-feature runtime facts. |
233	| Settings page shell | Connect form draft/update/reset to `settings` root public service. | Settings snapshot, recording/CSV preference selectors, validation result. | Startup settings load and later consumer notification may use runtime; settings rules stay in feature. |
234	| Storage/history local page shells | Connect local records/hour summaries/CSV material display to `storage-local-baseline` reader/service. | Storage read model, CSV material summaries, adapter operation result. | Required only for adapter construction, flush/shutdown or fake/platform replacement; pages must not create file/path adapters. |
235	| 404 page | Preserve catch-all user-visible route behavior. | Route metadata only. | Not required. |
236	
237	旧 `/connect` can be introduced as a route placeholder only after connection/platform design says what it may show. `/frames/send`、`/frames/receive`、`/scoe` should not be wired beyond explicit deferred navigation metadata until their feature designs exist.
238	
239	## 12. Deferred and blockers
240	
241	Deferred:
242	
243	- `/connect` real behavior and connection target/runtime state.
244	- `/frames/send`、`/frames/receive`、task/SCOE-specific page workflows.
245	- receive/send/task/SCOE/result/report/northbound internal designs.
246	- Status read model and global indicator semantics.
247	- Platform/preload/main API schema for file/path/dialog/window/transport.
248	- Packaged data path, long-running storage, high-speed storage short-circuit and performance strategy.
249	- HTTP/FTP, TestReport, file delivery, northbound external semantics and customer validation.
250	- Any auto-import policy beyond basic framework APIs.
251	
252	Blockers for implementation claims:
253	
254	- No runtime wiring can be claimed complete while `rewrite/src/runtime` remains only `.gitkeep`.
255	- No widgets contract can be claimed implemented while `rewrite/src/widgets` remains only `.gitkeep`.
256	- No UI page behavior can be claimed complete without manual checklist evidence after implementation.
257	- No platform, package, hardware or customer behavior can be claimed from this document.
258	- If future implementation needs feature internal subpath imports from pages/widgets/runtime, the blocker is a missing public API or misplaced UI boundary, not a reason to bypass the boundary.
259	
260	## 13. Review checklist for later implementation
261	
262	Later implementation or review should answer:
263	
264	1. Does every changed file list the correct owner: app, page, widget, feature component/composable, feature service/state/core, runtime or platform?
265	2. Do pages import only feature root public APIs, declared same-feature composables, runtime page APIs, widgets or shared?
266	3. Do widgets receive props/read-only snapshots instead of importing feature internals or services?
267	4. Does runtime create and wire services without keeping feature business truth?
268	5. Are feature service results converted into read models or UI snapshots before display?
269	6. Is Pinia only a provider or feature-local implementation detail, not a global business center?
270	7. Are validation levels recorded without upgrading static/fixture/manual evidence into runtime/package/hardware/customer claims?
271	8. Are deferred receive/send/task/SCOE/result/report/northbound domains still out of scope?
272

14	
15	# Rewrite feature boundaries
16	
17	## 1. Scope
18	
19	本轮是全局预设计阶段的 feature 边界与 owner 初筛，目的是在进入任何 `cs-feat-design` 前，先固定每个 feature 的职责边界、输入输出方向、运行事实归属、验证风险和明显的合并/拆分风险。
20	
21	Direct contract:
22	
23	- `AGENTS.md`
24	- `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-execution-charter.md`
25	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`
26	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-system-architecture.md`
27	- `codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
28	- `codestable/quality/rewrite-review-checklist.md`
29	
30	Boundary guards:
31	
32	- `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-northbound-overlap-and-gap-map.md`
33	- `easysdd/compound/2026-04-27-legacy-feature-inventory-and-oracle-map.md`
34	- 当前 `src` 和 `src-electron` 旧代码事实，只作为 evidence、oracle 候选和风险证据。
35	
36	Non-goals:
37	
38	- 不进入任何 feature 内部详细设计。
39	- 不写字段 schema、事件 payload、接口签名、northbound 字段、枚举或错误码。
40	- 不写 receive / send / task / SCOE 的详细实现。
41	- 不做代码移动，不实现业务代码。
42	- 不把旧页面、旧 store、旧 composable、旧 IPC、旧 main handler 组织自动升级为目标架构。
43	
44	Canonical rule:
45	
46	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md` 仍是目录、依赖方向和 feature 归口的 canonical 架构基线。本文只做 owner 初筛和后续 feature design 的入口材料。
47	
48	## 2. Legacy Evidence Summary
49	
50	Evidence:
51	
52	- 旧系统路由覆盖首页、连接、帧配置/编辑、发送、接收、设置、存储、历史和 SCOE 页面。`src/router/routes.ts:3-54`
53	- 左侧导航暴露首页、连接、SCOE、配置、发送、接收、存储、历史、设置。`src/components/layout/SidePanel.vue:32-47`
54	- 应用生命周期一次性装配 frame、send instance、receive config、serial、connection target、global stats、SCOE、history recording 等多个 store。`src/layouts/useAppLifecycle.ts:17-26`, `src/layouts/useAppLifecycle.ts:45-68`
55	- 串口和网络 store 接收事件后直接调用 `receiveFramesStore.handleReceivedData`。`src/stores/serialStore.ts:391-417`, `src/stores/netWorkStore.ts:200-213`
56	- `receiveFramesStore` 同时处理入站队列、SCOE 分支、receive API、全局统计、接收组当前值、表达式、星座图、frame stats 和发送触发条件。`src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:805-904`, `src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:1016-1205`, `src/stores/frames/receiveFramesStore.ts:1215-1293`
57	- send task 的本地任务状态、计时器、触发监听、进度缓存和状态索引聚集在 `sendTasksStore`，控制器停止任务时把停止写成 `completed`。`src/stores/frames/sendTasksStore.ts:15-30`, `src/stores/frames/sendTasksStore.ts:145-184`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useSendTaskController.ts:26-65`
58	- 统一发送器直接读取连接目标、发送实例统计、全局统计、表达式管理和 SCOE store。`src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:6-13`, `src/composables/frames/sendFrame/useUnifiedSender.ts:55-167`
59	- SCOE store 同时管理配置、SCOE TCP/UDP 连接、SCOE 发送、状态循环和测试工具记录。`src/stores/scoeStore.ts:18-39`, `src/stores/scoeStore.ts:212-340`
60	- 旧 Electron 配置已是 `contextIsolation: true`、`nodeIntegration: false`、`sandbox: false`，但 preload 暴露统一 `window.electron` 能力包，renderer `api/common` 直接围绕 `window.electron` 组织平台能力和部分业务语义。`src-electron/main/window.ts:28-39`, `src-electron/preload/index.ts:1-10`, `src-electron/preload/api/index.ts:1-31`, `src/api/common/index.ts:6-34`
61	- main 进程中 `receiveHandlers` 直接执行帧匹配、字段处理、统计结果构建，`networkHandlers` 中高速存储规则命中后会短路普通 renderer data event。`src-electron/main/ipc/receiveHandlers.ts:18-25`, `src-electron/main/ipc/receiveHandlers.ts:53-140`, `src-electron/main/ipc/networkHandlers.ts:498-520`
62	- 当前代码未形成独立 `northbound` store/composable/page，`result` 和 `report` 也没有独立 feature 入口；历史分析和 data display 是 `display` feature 的 legacy evidence/oracle 候选，CSV 和 high-speed storage 仍归 storage/receive/runtime 边界材料。
63	
64	Inference:
65	
66	- 旧系统页面入口应默认保留为用户可见入口，但页面结构不决定新 feature owner。
67	- 新系统必须把静态资产、运行事实、统计 read model、UI snapshot、平台能力和对外交付分开，不继续沿用旧 store 跨写。
68	- `result / report / northbound` 必须作为新边界补齐；不能用旧 `history / CSV / local export` 直接替代。
69	
70	## 3. Feature Boundary Table
71	
72	| Feature | Owner responsibilities | Does not own | Main inputs | Main outputs | Runtime fact owner | Static definitions / assets | Public API likely | Runtime orchestration likely | Platform/main/preload boundary | Validation posture | Initial scope class |
73	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
74	| `frame` | 帧资产、字段编辑、校验、序列化、导入导出、旧 JSON 迁移入口、frame fixture/oracle 材料 | 接收运行状态、发送执行状态、SCOE 命令流程、统计事实、报告交付 | 用户编辑、旧 JSON/导入文件、storage 读取、settings 默认值 | frame definition、field definition、可供 receive/send/SCOE/report 读取的静态资产 | 只拥有 frame 静态资产事实；不拥有命中次数、最近值、错误数 | 是。frame、field、frame-owned 展示元数据、legacy JSON migration 都涉及 | 是。receive/send/task/SCOE/report 需要只读读取 frame 资产或校验入口 | 通常不需要；导入迁移和跨 feature 装配可由 runtime 调用公开入口 | 文件导入导出经 storage/platform，不直接触 Electron | fixture/oracle 优先；导入导出和旧 JSON 迁移需要手工 checklist | `preserve` for visible asset behavior; legacy JSON internal shape is `deferred/candidate compatibility` |
75	| `connection` | 串口/TCP/TCP server/UDP 连接模型、连接实例、连接状态、target 路由、transport availability | 字节解析、业务结果、报告、任务生命周期、northbound deviceId | 平台串口/网络资源、用户连接操作、settings 默认连接参数 | target snapshot、connection status、transport send/receive availability、连接事件 | 连接实例和 transport 状态事实 | 连接配置可能持久化；不是业务设备身份资产 | 是。send/SCOE/storage/status/runtime 需要 target 查询和发送落点 | 是。启动/释放连接、SCOE 固定连接、跨 feature target 装配需要 runtime | 强涉及 platform/main/preload；main 只做平台 IO 和必要缓冲 | 串口/TCP/UDP 必须 hardware/runtime validation；静态只能说明边界 | `preserve` |
76	| `receive` | 输入字节流承接、batch/queue、帧匹配、字段解析、表达式输入、接收结果输出、receive fixture/oracle | 任务是否开始或结束、SCOE 完整领域执行、历史落盘编排、状态指示定义、报告语义 | connection 入站 bytes、frame 静态资产、receive 配置、SCOE 显式入口输入 | receive result、field value delta、parse error、match stats、给 task/status/result/storage/SCOE 的显式结果 | receive 解析运行事实和接收 read model；统计 owner 需在 design 中细化 | receive mapping/config 可能涉及静态配置；不得写回 frame definition | 是。task/status/result/storage/report 需要只读结果或事件 | 是。高频数据、SCOE 分支和 result/storage/status 消费需要 runtime 明确路由 | 强涉及 platform 数据流，但 core 不依赖 Electron；main 只能缓冲/批处理 | 自动 fixture 最高优先；真实串口/TCP/UDP 和高频链路需 runtime/hardware validation | `preserve` |
77	| `send` | 单帧发送（SendRequest→构帧→target 路由→transport write→SendResult）、发送队列（transport-level FIFO）、target 落地、发送统计 read model、send fixture/oracle | 任务编排/step 序列执行、SCOE 成功条件、报告交付、northbound 回执语义、定时/触发/序列调度 | frame/send instance、task send-step 请求、页面手动发送请求、connection target、settings | send result（SendResult）、bytes sent、send error、给 task/status/storage 的显式结果 | send 执行局部事实和发送结果事实；不拥有 task 主状态 | send instance/template 是静态或半静态资产，需要与 frame 分清 | 是。task/pages/runtime 需要发起发送请求 | 是。task send-step 调用 send 公开 service | 通过 connection/platform 落地；renderer 不直连串口/网络 | send build 可 fixture；真实 target 落地需 hardware/runtime validation | `preserve` |
78	| `task` | 通用执行引擎（接收执行计划 step 序列，按顺序执行，处理错误，追踪进度）、多态 step（send/wait-condition/delay）、多触发来源入口适配（用户 UI、定时器、receive 触发、SCOE 命令、northbound 外部命令）、条件匹配逻辑、调度、取消/停止/暂停/恢复语义 | 页面工作台状态、send 局部发送细节、receive 解析、northbound 对外回执协议、报告文件生成、SCOE 领域规则 | TaskDefinition（来自用户 UI / SCOE adapter / northbound adapter / 定时器）、send public service result、receive public API 事件、settings | TaskInstanceState、TaskProgress、TaskInstanceCompletion、TaskStepResult、给 result/status/northbound 的显式事件 | task/case/step lifecycle 主事实和执行进度事实；旧 send task 只能作为 local oracle | task config 或 legacy send task config 属兼容候选；不直接等同 northbound task schema | 是。pages/northbound/result/report/status/runtime 都需要 task 查询或命令入口 | 是。task 是通用执行引擎，跨 receive/send/result/report/northbound 的核心编排消费者和生产者 | timer 可以经 platform/main，但状态推进必须在 TS core/service | 定时/触发/SCOE 命令可 fixture；stop/pause/resume 和真实发送链需 runtime validation | `preserve` for timed send/local visible behavior; northbound task semantics are `deferred` |
79	| `scoe` | SCOE 协议、命令解析（指令码→step 定义映射）、完成条件定义、确认帧配置、SCOE 领域状态（commandSuccessCount 等）、SCOE 静态资产（卫星配置、SCOE 帧实例）、固定 source/target 声明、测试工具记录、SCOE fixture/oracle | 通用执行引擎（task owns）、单帧发送（send owns）、通用 receive/send 主链定义权、统一运行主状态、当前甲方 northbound 身份 | SCOE TCP server 入站字节（经 connection transport event，bypass receive）、SCOE config、frame/SCOE frame instance、task lifecycle 事件（TaskInstanceCompletion） | ScoeDomainState、TaskDefinition（翻译自 ScoeCommand，triggerSource=scoe-command）、SCOE 静态资产 | SCOE 领域状态和测试工具记录；不成为系统主状态 | 是。卫星/SCOE config、命令、参数、完成条件等静态定义 | 是。pages/runtime/task 需要显式入口 | 是。固定连接声明、命令翻译为 TaskDefinition、边界例外需要 runtime 登记 | 强涉及 connection/platform；main 不拥有 SCOE 协议语义 | fixture + real SCOE device validation；无设备只能静态/fixture | `preserve`, with `deferred` hardware proof |
80	| `storage` | 本地持久化、history、CSV、高速存储、迁移脚本输入输出、文件素材归档 | northbound 文件回传协议、任务结果事实定义、报告对象语义 | feature 静态资产、receive/result/report 素材、高速 bytes、settings、platform path/file API | persisted assets、history records、CSV/local files、report 素材文件、高速存储文件 | storage/history/file 写入事实；不定义 result/report 成功语义 | 是。本地模板、旧 JSON、history/CSV、高速规则和迁移输入输出 | 是。frame/settings/report/result/pages 需要存取能力 | 是。打包态 data path、高速存储短路、报告素材归档需 runtime 登记 | 强涉及 main/path/fs/preload；main 只做文件 IO、缓冲和安全路径能力 | fixture + manual checklist；打包态 data path 和高频存储必须 runtime validation | `preserve` |
81	| `settings` | 设置模型、默认值、持久化、设置页可见配置、跨 feature 配置分发边界 | 领域运行事实、任务推进、状态事实、平台能力 | 用户设置、默认值、storage 读取、feature design 中声明的配置项 | typed settings snapshot、feature-specific config inputs | settings 只拥有配置事实；不拥有运行状态 | 是。默认值、用户设置、配置导入导出 | 是。features 读取配置快照或订阅变更 | 通常不需要；启动注入和跨 feature 默认值可 runtime 装配 | 通过 storage/platform 持久化，不直接触 main | fixture + manual checklist；配置持久化路径需 runtime/package check | `preserve` |
82	| `status` | 状态指示、健康状态、状态视图、内部状态摘要、UI-facing read model | 心跳协议本身、任务主状态事实、receive/send 原始事实、northbound 外部状态枚举 | connection/task/receive/send/SCOE/result 的只读 snapshot、settings 指示灯配置 | status view、health summary、indicator snapshot、给 northbound 的内部状态素材 | status read model；不拥有底层事实 | 指示灯配置属于 status/settings 交叉，需在 design 定 owner | 是。pages/widgets/northbound 需要状态摘要 | 是。跨 feature 状态聚合应由 runtime 或 status service 读取公开入口 | 不直接触 main；心跳归 northbound/platform，不在 status 内实现协议 | fixture + manual checklist；硬件状态需 runtime/hardware validation | `preserve` for local indicators; external heartbeat is `deferred` |
